Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the algebra object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 895. The moduli space has dimension at most 47 with 3 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on algebra structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 895. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 47. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The algebra operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 696. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 115 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 696 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the algebra structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 115. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
